<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

Now I need to design item 8: 确认问题后自动改简历并导出 PDF/DOC.

Plan:
- shared/revise.ts done (applyRevisions, etc.)
- server: store a revised resume per session. Options: store revisions accepted in meta or a new table. Simpler: add table `revision` (session_id, claim_id, start, end, before, after, accepted_at) or store in meta `revisions`. Let me consider: 需要导出 PDF/DOC of revised resume. Need API:
  - POST /api/sessions/:id/revisions (body: claimIds[] or all) → apply revisions, return {text, applied, skipped, digest}
  - GET /api/sessions/:id/resume.docx? 生成 Word (.doc via HTML) and PDF (print HTML) of revised resume.

Given earlier ADR-0006: 服务端不自己生成 PDF，走可打印 HTML + window.print(). So "导出 PDF 或 DOC" = 可打印 HTML (?print=1) + .doc. For resume, produce a resume HTML with highlights of changes and a "copy" version without highlights.

Let me think about what user asked (item 8): "提出问题后，用户确认后应自动把简历做对应修改，并提供 PDF 或 DOC 导出". So: after verdicts/suggestions, user checks which suggestions to accept → click "确认修改" → the resume text updated → export PDF/DOC.

Implementation plan:
1. shared/revise.ts ✅
2. server store: persist accepted revisions in session meta? Better a table for auditability. But simpler and consistent: store in `meta.revision = {digest, accepted: claimIds[], text?}` — but text duplicates resume. Actually session has resume_text column. We can add a `revised_resume` column? Schema v5 adding table `revision`:
   CREATE TABLE revision (session_id, claim_id, start, end, before, after, accepted_at, PRIMARY KEY(session_id, claim_id))
   And revised text computed on the fly by applyRevisions(session.resumeText, revisions).
   That's clean and reconstructible: text is derived, not stored (mirrors "事件是真相" philosophy).

3. HTTP:
   - POST /api/sessions/:id/revisions {claimIds: string[]} → 采纳这些建议（默认全部 suggested）→ save → 返回 {text, applied, skipped, digest}
   - DELETE /api/sessions/:id/revisions/:claimId → 取消采纳
   - GET /api/sessions/:id/resume.md / resume.html?print=1 / resume.doc → 导出改后简历
4. web: 在 ClaimBoard 的每条建议旁加「采纳这条」勾选；顶部或报告区加「导出改后简历 PDF / Word」按钮；提供「预览改后简历」。
5. tests, e2e, screenshots, docs, commits.

<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

Now let me think about the design for item 8: 确认问题后自动改简历并导出 PDF/DOC。

Requirement (m04531 #8): "提出问题后，用户确认后应自动把简历做对应修改，并提供 PDF 或 DOC 导出"

Design:
- Frontend: in ClaimBoard, each suggestion gets "采纳" (accept) button. Accepted set stored in App state (per session). Then a panel/dialog shows the revised resume preview with diffs highlighted, and export buttons: 导出 PDF / 导出 Word / 复制文本 / 下载 .txt / .md.
- Server: POST /api/sessions/:id/revise with `{ accepted: string[] }` → returns `{ text, applied, skipped, digest, pending }`. And export endpoints: `GET /api/sessions/:id/revised.doc` (Word HTML) and `GET /api/sessions/:id/revised.txt`? For PDF, following ADR-0006 we don't generate PDF server-side; we provide printable HTML with `?print=1` that triggers window.print() → "另存为 PDF". That's consistent with existing report.html.

So:
- POST /api/sessions/:id/revise (JSON body: claimIds) → revision plan preview (text + applied + skipped + pendingClaimIds + digest). Not persisted? Requirement says "确认后自动把简历做对应修改，并提供导出". Persisting accepted revisions per session would be good (so history keeps it). But minimal: compute on the fly from session's stored suggestions; that's deterministic. However for export endpoints (doc/html), the server needs to know which claims were accepted — pass them as query `?accepted=C1,C2` so no new table needed. That's simpler and keeps statelessness. But then the frontend must pass accepted list to export URL. That's fine.

Hmm, but persisting is nicer for "回放可复现". Let me think about scale of work: I still have items 7, 10, 11, 12 to do. Keep item 8 reasonably scoped but complete:

Server:
1. `packages/server/src/revise-session.ts` (or put in pipeline): `reviseSession(db, sessionId, acceptedIds)` → gather suggestions from events (assembleSession gives suggestions), build Revision[] via revisionOf, apply, return {text, applied, skipped, pending, digest, source digest}.
2. HTTP:
   - `POST /api/sessions/:id/revise` body `{accepted: string[]}` → JSON result (also `resumeText`? no — big. Actually the revised text is needed by frontend to preview; resume is ~1000 chars so fine).
   - `GET /api/sessions/:id/revised.txt` ?accepted=... → text/plain attachment
   - `GET /api/sessions/:id/revised.doc` ?accepted=... → application/msword with HTML (reuse a renderer that shows revised resume with highlights)
   - `GET /api/sessions/:id/revised.html` ?accepted=...&print=1 → printable HTML for PDF (另存为 PDF)
   - Maybe `GET /api/sessions/:id/revised.md`.
3. Renderer: `packages/server/src/revise-html.ts` — renders revised resume document with the changed spans highlighted (use movedSpans), plus a change list.

Web:
- ClaimBoard: add 采纳/取消采纳 toggle per suggestion (checkbox), props `accepted: Set<string>` and `onToggleAccept`.
- New component `RevisePanel.tsx` (dialog/overlay): shows revised resume preview with changed parts highlighted, applied/skipped list, buttons 导出 PDF（打印）/ 导出 Word / 复制全文 / 下载 .txt, and digest.
- App.tsx: state `accepted: string[]`, reset on new session/load; `openRevise` etc.

Tests:
- shared revise.test.ts (done, 11)
- server test: revise-session + HTTP endpoints
- web test: maybe small — revise helpers (e.g. accepted toggle order, digest?). Keep light: a test for `reviseRequest` building? Could add web test for a pure helper `acceptedOrder`/`summarize`.

e2e: add scenario: after a run, accept one suggestion, open revise panel, assert text contains after content, download link exists... Downloading in CDP is messy; just assert href and click? Better assert that clicking 导出 PDF opens report? Keep to asserting panel contents and that the export URLs return 200 via fetch inside page? Simpler: assert buttons exist and one `fetch` of the doc endpoint returns 200 (can do via Runtime.evaluate fetch). Hmm e2e uses raw CDP; simpler to assert UI elements and then check endpoint via a separate HTTP fetch from Node (the e2e script has the base URL). That's clean: after the run, call fetch(base + '/api/sessions/' + id + '/revised.doc?accepted=C1') and assert 200 + content-type.

```text
1 /**
2  * 把一盘沙盘渲染成一份可以直接打印的 HTML 报告。
3  *
4  * 与 report.ts 的关系：同一份事实（SessionView）的两种呈现。
5  *   - Markdown 是能带走、能 diff、能进仓库的形态；

8  * 而这份报告需要锚点引用块、判定配色与打印样式。两边读的是同一个 SessionView，
9  * 事实只有一个来源；多写一个薄呈现层，好过让服务端去依赖一个工具目录。
10  */
```

- After a session has run, the user sees suggestions (改写建议). Add "采纳" (accept) checkboxes on each suggestion. Then a "生成改好的简历" action that:
  - POST /api/sessions/:id/revise with `{ claimIds: [...] }` → server loads session resume text + suggestions, builds Revisions, applies via applyRevisions, returns `{ text, applied, skipped, digest }`.
  - Also GET /api/sessions/:id/revised.md / .html / .doc for download in PDF/DOC.
- Persist accepted revisions? Better: store accepted revision record in meta so report can include it, and downloads are stable. Requirement says "提出问题后，用户确认后应自动把简历做对应修改，并提供 PDF 或 DOC 导出". So flow: confirm → resume revised → export PDF/DOC.

Implementation plan:
1. shared/revise.ts done (11 tests green).
2. Server: new module `packages/server/src/revise.ts`? Better: `packages/server/src/resume-doc.ts` for rendering revised resume to HTML (printable, for PDF) and .doc. And `revisionsOfSession(view)` helper to build Revisions from view.suggestions.
3. HTTP: 
   - `POST /api/sessions/:id/revise` body `{claimIds?: string[]}` → returns `{applied, skipped, text, digest, accepted}`; persists accepted list into meta (`meta.revisions = {claimIds, digest, at}`) so downloads work.
   - `GET /api/sessions/:id/resume.html?print=1` and `.doc` → renders revised resume (if meta.revisions exists use stored accepted set; else 400 with hint).
   Maybe simpler: downloads use stored meta. Add `GET /api/sessions/:id/resume.md` too? Keep to html (PDF) + doc.
4. Web: in ClaimBoard suggestion block add checkbox "采纳这条改写" with data-shot `accept-<claimId>`; App-level state `accepted: Set<string>`; a bar/button "确认采纳并生成新简历" data-shot `revise-run`; after success show a new pane/dialog with revised text + highlight + download buttons `revised-pdf`, `revised-doc`, and "写回投料台" button.

Given the layout (four columns), maybe show the revised resume in a modal overlay (like InterviewerStudio) — call it `RevisionPanel` / `RevisionPreview`. That's cleaner and testable.

Where to put accept checkboxes: suggestions are in ClaimBoard (middle column). Also add a top-bar entry "改简历" appears when there are suggestions.

Let me design the server API concretely:

- `POST /api/sessions/:id/revise` body `{ claimIds: string[] }`:
  - session must exist; must have run (suggestions exist).
  - filter suggestions by claimIds (only status==='suggested'); unknown → 400.
  - build revisions via revisionOf; applyRevisions(session.resumeText, revisions).
  - persist `meta.revisions = { claimIds, digest: reviseDigest(text), applied: [...], at }`? Storing applied list is useful for report. Keep meta small: claimIds + digest + at.
  - return `{ text, applied, skipped, digest, accepted }`.
- `GET /api/sessions/:id/resume.html` (+ `?print=1`) and `GET /api/sessions/:id/resume.doc`:
  - recompute from meta.revisions.claimIds; if none → 400 "还没有确认采纳任何改写".
  - render printable HTML of resume text with accepted changes highlighted? For export we want clean resume text (that's the point: a resume you can send). Highlighting in exported PDF would be bad. So export = plain formatted resume text; the in-app preview shows the diff.

So renderer: `renderResumeHtml(text, {title, print})` — simple: paragraphs from lines, A4 page CSS, plus small footer "由 CrossExam 生成"? Better not to add branding to a resume... but a small footer is fine and honest. Actually a resume exported for sending shouldn't have "generated by" watermark. 15   text: string;

```text
19 export function clampRange(range: Range, length: number): Range | null {
20   const start = Math.max(0, Math.min(range.start, length));
21   const end = Math.max(0, Math.min(range.end, length));
22   return end > start ? { start, end } : null;
23 }
24 
25 /** 把重叠或相邻的区间并起来，避免在原文上叠出一堆同色小块 */
26 export function mergeRanges(ranges: Range[]): Range[] {
27   const sorted = [...ranges].sort((a, b) => a.start - b.start || a.end - b.end);
28   const merged: Range[] = [];
29   for (const range of sorted) {
30     const last = merged[merged.length - 1];
31     if (last !== undefined && range.start <= last.end) {
32       if (range.end > last.end) last.end = range.end;
33       continue;
34     }
35     merged.push({ start: range.start, end: range.end });
36   }
37   return merged;
38 }
```

<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

Now proceed with server side of item 8: HTTP endpoints for revision apply + export. Let me think about the design.

Item 8: "提出问题后，用户确认后应自动把简历做对应修改，并提供 PDF 或 DOC 导出" — user confirms suggestions, resume gets modified, export PDF/DOC.

Design:
- Server: `POST /api/sessions/:id/revise` with body `{ claimIds: string[] }` → returns `{ text, applied: Revision[], skipped: RevisionSkip[], digest }`. Pure computation from stored suggestions, no LLM.
- Export: `POST /api/revised/resume.doc` / `.html`? Since server doesn't generate PDF (ADR-0006), use the same trick: HTML → print to PDF, and .doc via application/msword.
- Better: `POST /api/sessions/:id/resume-revised.html?print=1` — but POST can't be a link. Hmm. The front end holds the revised text in memory; to export we need a POST that returns the file, triggered via fetch → blob → download. That works in browser.

Let me design endpoints:
- `POST /api/sessions/:id/revise` body `{ claimIds?: string[], text?: string }` → JSON `{ text, applied, skipped, pending, digest }`. Actually should we allow arbitrary text? Keep it: revise against the session's stored resumeText only (server truth), claimIds selects which suggestions to apply.
- `POST /api/sessions/:id/resume.docx` — hmm docx generation needs zip writer. We already have zip *reader* in import/zip.ts. Writing a .docx requires deflateRawSync — Node has zlib.deflateRawSync. That's doable but a fair amount of work. Alternative: reuse the HTML approach: `.doc` with application/msword, which Word opens natively. That's already what report.doc does. That satisfies "PDF 或 DOC 导出" — .doc + HTML print-to-PDF.

Actually generating a real .docx would be nicer but risky/time-consuming. But wait — we have the ability to write a docx with zlib deflateRawSync; minimal docx = [Content_Types].xml, _rels/.rels, word/document.xml. That's ~80 lines. Hmm. But .doc via HTML is already accepted practice in this repo (report.doc). Consistency argues for reusing that path.

However, a real .docx would be a stronger deliverable... but the risk: Word may complain about minimal docx. HTML-as-.doc is well-trodden. And PDF via browser print is exactly what report PDF does. I'll go with HTML + .doc + print PDF, consistent with ADR-0006.

So:
- `packages/server/src/resume-html.ts`: `renderResumeHtml({ title, text, applied, skipped, options })` — a print-friendly resume document with highlighted changes (movedSpans) and an appendix listing what changed and what was skipped. Plus `resumeBaseName(sessionId)`, `resumeHtmlFileName`, `resumeDocFileName`, and maybe a markdown version.
- Endpoints:
  - `POST /api/sessions/:id/revise` → JSON (needs body claimIds). Returns text + spans + skipped + pending.
  - `POST /api/sessions/:id/resume.md|html|doc` — need POST because body carries chosen claimIds. Front-end fetches blob and triggers download.
  
Hmm, but also nice: GET variants with `?claims=C1,C2` so links work directly. That's simpler for the front end (plain `<a>` download). But URL length limits for many claims — max 12 claims, fine.

Let me do GET with `?claims=` query: `GET /api/sessions/:id/resume.md?claims=C1,C2`, `.html` (+`?print=1`), `.doc`. And `POST /api/sessions/:id/revise` for the preview JSON that the UI shows live.

Actually the UI needs live preview of revised text as user toggles checkboxes. Client-side could do it, but the logic lives in shared/revise.ts — and the web package doesn't import shared (D11: node:crypto dependency). Hmm! That's a problem: `revise.ts` imports `shortHash` from util.ts which imports node:crypto. So web can't import it.

Options: (a) add a subpath export `@crossexam/shared/revise.ts` like domain.ts — but revise.ts imports util.ts → node:crypto → breaks in browser. (b) Do preview server-side via POST /api/sessions/:id/revise on each toggle (debounced). That keeps one implementation, no duplication. Latency is local, fine.

I'll go with (b): server-side preview endpoint. Cleaner and honors D11.

Now I need to plan the "简历修订" (resume revision) feature: user confirms suggestions → auto-apply revisions to resume → export as PDF/DOC.

Design:
1. Server: store revision state. Where? Options:
   - New table `revision` per session (session_id, claim_id, start, end, before, after, applied_at).
   - Or store in session meta.
   
   Given AGENTS.md schema discipline, a table is cleaner. But we need schema v5. Let's think about what's minimal and correct.

Actually, thinking about scope: the user's requirement #8: "提出问题后，用户确认后应自动把简历做对应修改，并提供 PDF 或 DOC 导出". So: after the run, for each suggestion, user can accept/reject. Accepted ones get applied to the resume text. Then export the revised resume as PDF/DOC.

Implementation plan:
- shared/revise.ts: already has applyRevisions, movedSpans, revisionOf, reviseDigest, pendingClaimIds.
- Server: new table `revision` (schema v5): session_id, claim_id, start, end, before, after, accepted INTEGER, created_at; PK (session_id, claim_id).
  Actually maybe simpler: keep revisions in the session meta? But meta is JSON; we need per-claim accept toggles. A table is the right projection, and event table already is the source of truth.
  
  Hmm. Simpler and consistent with existing design: append events of kind `revision` with payload {claimId, accepted}. And a table for query convenience. That's the existing pattern (event = source of truth, tables = projection). But that adds a lot of code.

Let me consider: the existing pattern for verdicts/suggestions is: they're stored in the claim table (advice_json) and also emitted as events. The pipeline persists results in one transaction.

For revisions (user decisions), the cleanest minimal approach:
- Table `revision` with PK (session_id, claim_id): start, end, before, after, accepted INTEGER (0/1), decided_at.
- On accept: POST /api/sessions/:id/revisions {claimId, accepted}. Server computes revisionOf from the stored suggestion in claim table, validates via applyRevisions, saves.
- GET /api/sessions/:id/revisions → { revisions: [{claimId, accepted, before, after, start, end}], resumeText (revised), digest, skipped }.
- Export: GET /api/sessions/:id/resume.html?print=1, /resume.doc, /resume.txt — revised resume with highlights.

Hmm, but do we need to persist accept decisions at all? Alternative: keep it entirely client-side — the frontend has the suggestions, applies revisions locally with shared/revise.ts, and posts the resulting text to a render endpoint. That's much less code. But: (a) 复现 requires the decision be recorded; (b) page refresh loses it.

Given the competition is about 过程可复现性 (25%), persisting decisions matters. But I also need to balance effort — remaining items 7, 10, 11, 12 still to do.

Middle ground: persist decisions in a `revision` table (small), append an event for the record, and have the resume export read from DB. That's the honest design. Let's do it but keep it tight.

Actually, let me reconsider a much simpler persistence: store decisions in session meta? `meta.revisions: {claimId: 'accepted'|'rejected'}`. saveSessionMeta already exists. Hmm, but meta is a bag; schema'd table is cleaner and queryable. But schema v5 migration + repo functions + tests... it's maybe 80 lines. Acceptable.

Let me decide: **schema v5 with a `revision` table**.

Columns: session_id, claim_id, start, end, before, after, accepted INTEGER NOT NULL, created_at TEXT NOT NULL, updated_at? Keep: decided_at TEXT NOT NULL. PK (session_id, claim_id), FK to claim.

Repo functions:
- `saveRevision(db, sessionId, rev: Revision & {accepted: boolean}, at?)` — upsert.
- `listRevisions(db, sessionId): RevisionRow[]`
- `deleteRevision(db, sessionId, claimId): boolean`
- `clearRunArtifacts` should also delete revisions (since claims get deleted).

Wait — revisions FK to claim(session_id, claim_id) ON DELETE CASCADE, so clearRunArtifacts deleting claims will cascade. Good.

Pipeline: add `revisions: RevisionRow[]` to SessionView, and computed `revised: {text, digest, applied, skipped, pending}`. Where to compute? In assembleSession — compute revised resume text from session.resumeText + accepted revisions via applyRevisions.

HTTP:
- `POST /api/sessions/:id/revisions` body `{claimId, accepted}` → validates claim has suggestion, computes revisionOf(claimId, suggestion), applyRevisions against current accepted set (to detect conflict/overlap), saves. Return full revision state.
- `DELETE /api/sessions/:id/revisions/:claimId` → undo decision.
- `GET /api/sessions/:id/resume.txt` / `.html` (`?print=1`) / `.doc` → revised resume, with highlights for accepted changes (movedSpans).

Frontend:
- In ClaimBoard suggestion block, add 「采纳这条改写」/「不采纳」 buttons (data-shot=revise-accept-<claimId>, revise-reject-...).
- New pane or a section showing the revised resume + export buttons. Where? The ResumePane shows original with focus. Maybe add a toggle in ResumePane header: 原文 / 改后. And header buttons for 简历 PDF / 简历 Word / 简历 .md? Requirement says "提供 PDF 或 DOC 导出" — so 简历 PDF + 简历 DOC.

Simplest good UX: ResumePane gets a tab switcher (原文 / 改后（N 处）) and export buttons appear when there's at least one accepted revision.

```text
1 /**
2  * 采纳改写建议：把「改前 / 改后」真正落到简历文本上。
3  *
4  * 三条硬约束，写在代码里而不是写在文档里：
5  *
6  * 1. **只认原文区间，不认客户端传来的文本。** 采纳请求只带 claimId，改前改后
7  *    一律从数据库里那份建议读出来；否则「把简历改一遍」就变成了一个接受任意
8  *    字符串替换的接口。
9  * 2. **区间必须落在原文上。** 用 isGrounded 复核一次，锚不上的建议一律跳过并
10  *    给出理由，绝不「按记忆里的位置硬替换」——一次错位会把整份简历改乱。
11  * 3. **倒序应用。** 一条建议改完，它后面的所有字符位置都会平移。从后往前改，
12  *    前面的区间就不用重算；同时用一个右边界护栏挡住区间重叠的两条建议，
13  *    保证结果与「先改哪条」无关。
14  */

21 export interface Revision {
```

Now let me plan item 8 (确认问题后自动改简历并导出 PDF/DOC).

Design:
- After a run, the user sees suggestions per claim. They can "采纳" (accept) each suggestion. Accepted suggestions are applied to the resume text via `applyRevisions`, producing a revised resume text + digest. Then export as PDF/DOC/HTML/MD.
- Persistence: store revisions in DB so it survives reload? Best: a `revision` table (schema v5) recording accepted claimIds per session, plus the derived revised text? The derived text is recomputable from resume_text + accepted claimIds + suggestions. But suggestions live in claim.advice_json. So store only accepted claimIds. That's clean and honest: no duplicate text.

But careful: revisions must be stable — if the session re-runs, suggestions change, claimIds regenerate. Accepting then re-running... `clearRunArtifacts` deletes claims/events. So accepted claimIds become dangling. We can handle: on run, clear revisions too? Or filter out unknown claimIds when rendering. Simpler: store accepted rows referencing (session_id, claim_id) with FK to claim — cascade delete on claim delete. That means re-running clears them automatically. Good design and self-documenting.

Schema v5: table `revision (session_id, claim_id, created_at, PRIMARY KEY(session_id, claim_id), FOREIGN KEY (session_id, claim_id) REFERENCES claim(session_id, id) ON DELETE CASCADE)`.

Then HTTP:
- `POST /api/sessions/:id/revisions` body `{claimIds: string[]}` → replace the accepted set (or add?). I think: `PUT`-like semantics = set exactly. Let's use `POST /api/sessions/:id/revisions` with `{ accepted: string[] }` = replace set. Returns `RevisionView` = `{ accepted: string[], result: {text, applied, skipped}, digest, pending: string[], html? }`. Hmm — keep it lean: return the revision state view.
- `GET /api/sessions/:id/revisions` → same view.
- Export: `GET /api/sessions/:id/resume.html` (printable), `.doc`, `.md`, `.txt`? User asked PDF or DOC export. PDF via browser print (same pattern as report: HTML + ?print=1). DOC via HTML + application/msword.

Also need: the revised resume shown in UI with diff highlighting. Use `movedSpans` to highlight new content in the revised text. ResumePane takes text/focus/hits — we can pass the revised text with hits = movedSpans ranges. But segments kinds are plain/hit/focus; hits render as highlight. Good enough: revised spans as `hit`.

Where in UI? Add to ClaimBoard's suggestion block a checkbox "采纳" per suggestion. And a new panel "改写与导出" — maybe in the header or a new right pane section. Simplest robust: add a "改写台" section into the ResumePane column: a toggle between 原文 / 改写后, plus buttons 导出 PDF / Word / Markdown, plus count of pending.

Plus store the revised text? We can recompute deterministically from resume_text + accepted revisions. Store accepted claimIds. Actually the final revised text = applyRevisions(resumeText, revisions of accepted claims). Deterministic, so we only need the accepted set. But storing the resulting text + digest is useful for export stability. Hmm — recomputing is fine and keeps a single source of truth. But if resume_text changes... it doesn't. I'll store revision rows (accepted) and compute text on demand; also store digest in a `revision` meta for display.

Let me think about minimal but solid: new table `revision`:
```
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS revision (
  session_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES session(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  claim_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  start INTEGER NOT NULL,
  end INTEGER NOT NULL,
  before TEXT NOT NULL,
  after TEXT NOT NULL,
  created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (session_id, claim_id)
)
```
v5 migration = just add table.

2. HTTP:
- `GET /api/sessions/:id/resume` → `{ original, revised, applied: [{claimId,start,end,before,after}], skipped: [...], digest, pending: [claimIds] }` (POST?) Let's do GET with optional `?accepted=C1,C2` — no, accepted lives in DB. Actually the flow: user clicks "采纳" per claim → POST /api/sessions/:id/revisions {claimId} → server reads the suggestion from claim.advice_json, builds Revision, validates via applyRevisions, inserts row. DELETE to un-accept. GET /revisions returns the assembled view.
- `GET /api/sessions/:id/resume.html` → printable HTML of the revised resume (with highlight + change list). `?print=1`.
- `GET /api/sessions/:id/resume.doc` → same HTML + application/msword.
- `GET /api/sessions/:id/resume.txt` → plain text download? Useful. Maybe include.

For PDF: ADR-0006 says server doesn't generate PDF itself; it hands a printable HTML and the browser prints. So "提供 PDF 或 DOC 导出" = 导出的 HTML 带 print（浏览器另存为 PDF）+ .doc（Word 打开另存为 PDF）。That's consistent with existing report behavior. Good — reuse.

Actually a dedicated table is cleaner and matches "event is truth" philosophy... but simpler: store in meta as `revisions: { claimId: {start,end,before,after} }`. But recomputation from claim advice_json is deterministic: suggestion is stored on claim row (advice_json). So accepted set alone suffices — we can rebuild Revision objects from stored suggestions. That's a nice property: "只记采纳了哪几条，改写内容仍然来自库里那份建议".

So: `meta.revisions` = array of claimIds (strings). metaStrings already exists in pipeline for notices. We can add `metaAcceptedRevisions(meta)`.

Wait but if suggestion changes? It can't - suggestions are persisted once per run and clearRunArtifacts deletes claims on re-run. Fine.

But there's a subtlety: after re-run, accepted revisions referencing deleted claims would be dropped. Fine.

Actually I already have a good picture. For item 8, I need to design:

Feature 8: 确认问题后自动把简历做对应修改，并提供 PDF 或 DOC 导出。

Design:
- After a session runs, user sees suggestions per claim (before/after). Each suggestion has a checkbox "采纳" (accept). Accepted revisions applied via applyRevisions (shared). Then:
  - Server endpoint: POST /api/sessions/:id/revise with { accepted: claimId[] } → returns { text, applied, skipped, digest }
  - Server endpoint: POST /api/sessions/:id/revise/export?... 或 GET /api/sessions/:id/revised.doc / .html?print=1 → 导出手改后的简历为 PDF/DOC。
  
Simpler: persist the accepted set + revised text into session meta (or a new table), then export endpoints read it.

But careful about "自动把简历做对应修改" — must be safe: anchor-based replacement only where the suggestion's before text matches the original text verbatim; otherwise skip with reason. That's exactly what applyRevisions does.

Export as PDF: server doesn't generate PDF itself (ADR-0006). So provide print-ready HTML with ?print=1 → window.print() → save as PDF. And .doc for Word. Same pattern as report export. That's consistent with existing architecture. Good — item 8 asks "提供 PDF 或 DOC 导出", and the existing pattern already does "报告 PDF" via printable HTML. So revised resume gets `revised.html?print=1` and `revised.doc`.

Design:
- New table `revision`? Or store in session meta? Better: a table `revision` (session_id, claim_id, before, after, start, end, accepted_at) — global library? It's per-session.

Actually simpler and more in keeping: store accepted revisions in a table `revision` with PRIMARY KEY (session_id, claim_id), FK to session. Then a derived "revised resume text" computed on demand by applyRevisions(session.resumeText, revisions). Deterministic, no stored derived text (avoids divergence). Export endpoints:
- `GET /api/sessions/:id/resume.md` (plain text)
- `GET /api/sessions/:id/resume.doc` (Word via HTML)
- `GET /api/sessions/:id/resume.html` (printable, ?print=1)
- `POST /api/sessions/:id/revisions` body {claimIds: string[]} → apply, persist, return {text, applied, skipped, digest, pending}
- `DELETE /api/sessions/:id/revisions/:claimId` → 撤销
- `GET /api/sessions/:id/revisions` → list

```text
1 /**
2  * 表结构。
3  *
4  * 设计上的四个要点：
5  *
6  * 1. **event 表是只追加的事实来源。** 前端重连、复盘、导出证据链全都读它，
7  *    而不是去反推各张业务表。业务表是为了查询方便做的投影，可以重建。
8  *
9  * 2. **llm_call 表只记指纹与用量，不记提示词正文。** 正文在 fixtures 里，
10  *    表里只留 digest，于是「这次演示用了多少钱、有没有联网」可以统计，
11  *    而简历内容不会因为这张表多存一份。
12  *
13  * 3. **主张与追问的编号是「会话内编号」，不是全局编号。** C1、P1 由抽取器
14  *    每场重新数起，因此主键必须是 (session_id, id)。第一版把 id 单独做主键，
15  *    单场演示永远看不出问题，直到同一个进程里跑第二场会话才撞
16  *    UNIQUE constraint failed: claim.id —— 这正是 v2 迁移存在的原因。
17  *
18  * 4. **追问表不设「一轮一条」的唯一约束。** v2 曾经有
19  *    UNIQUE (session_id, claim_id, round, interviewer_id)，本意是防重复，
20  *    实际上和编排器允许的「一位面试官一轮问同一条主张两条」直接冲突：
21  *    第一问追口径、第二问追条件，产品上完全合理。冲突的后果不是拒绝插入，
22  *    而是整场事务在落库时回滚、接口 500。合成卡带里每条主张每轮恰好只有一问，
23  *    所以这个缺陷潜伏了很久，直到真模型一次给出两条才炸出来。v3 把该约束换成
24  *    普通索引：去重交给提问去重那道闸门（probe.ts 的 seenQuestions），
25  *    数据库不再替产品做它没想做的决定。
26  *
27  * 迁移步骤一律**冻结在自己的时点上**：DDL 是「现在」的结构，DDL_V2 是「v2 当时」
28  * 的结构。否则历史库升级时会跳过中间形态，一步到位建出最新表，看着没事，
29  * 但下一次改结构就没有可回退的落脚点。
30  */
```

```
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS revision (
  session_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES session(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  claim_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  start INTEGER NOT NULL,
  end INTEGER NOT NULL,
  before TEXT NOT NULL,
  after TEXT NOT NULL,
  accepted_at TEXT NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (session_id, claim_id)
)
```
Plus need ordering; we can order by start.

Then endpoints:
- `GET /api/sessions/:id/revised` → returns `{accepted, skipped, pending, text, digest, spans}` — recomputed from session.resumeText + stored revisions.
- `POST /api/sessions/:id/revisions` body `{claimIds: string[]}` → replace accepted set (idempotent), validate each claimId has a suggestion with status suggested, use revisionOf. Returns same view as GET.
- `DELETE /api/sessions/:id/revisions` → clear.
- `GET /api/sessions/:id/resume.html?print=1` and `/resume.doc` → render revised resume with highlights (movedSpans) as printable HTML, with print auto.

```text
294 export function migrate(db: Db): number {
295   const current = readVersion(db);
296 
297   if (current > SCHEMA_VERSION) {
298     throw new SchemaVersionError(
299       '数据库结构版本是 v' + String(current) + '，高于本代码支持的 v' + String(SCHEMA_VERSION) + '；请更新代码，不要用旧代码写新库。',
300     );
301   }
302   if (current === SCHEMA_VERSION) return current;
303 
304   if (current === 0) {
305     db.exec('BEGIN');
306     try {
307       for (const statement of DDL) db.exec(statement);
308       db.exec('PRAGMA user_version = ' + String(SCHEMA_VERSION));
309       db.exec('COMMIT');
310     } catch (err) {
311       db.exec('ROLLBACK');
312       throw err;
313     }
314     return SCHEMA_VERSION;
315   }
316 
317   for (let version = current + 1; version <= SCHEMA_VERSION; version += 1) {
318     const step = MIGRATIONS[version];
319     if (step === undefined) {
320       throw new SchemaVersionError('缺少从 v' + String(version - 1) + ' 到 v' + String(version) + ' 的迁移步骤。');
321     }
322     step(db);
323   }
324   return SCHEMA_VERSION;
325 }
```

```text
60  * 两段文本是否指的是同一段原文。

66 function sameText(actual: string, claimed: string): boolean {
67   if (actual === claimed) return true;
```

```ts
/**
 * 采纳改写：把用户确认过的建议真正落到简历上，并把结果留档。
 * ...
 */
import { applyRevisions, movedSpans, pendingClaimIds, revisionOf, reviseDigest } from '@crossexam/shared';
import type { Revision, RevisionSkip } from '@crossexam/shared';
import {
  clearRevisions, getRevisedResume, getSession, insertRevision, listClaims, saveRevisedResume, withTransaction,
} from './store/index.ts';
import type { Db } from './store/index.ts';

export interface RevisedView {
  sessionId: string;
  text: string;
  digest: string;
  /** 真正落地的改写（原文坐标） */
  accepted: Revision[];
  /** 落地之后在新文本里的位置，导出带高亮的简历要用 */
  spans: Revision[];
  /** 还有建议但这次没采纳的主张 */
  pending: string[];
  /** 采纳了但改不动的，带理由 */
  skipped: RevisionSkip[];
  createdAt: string;
  updatedAt: string;
}

export class ReviseError extends Error {
  constructor(message: string, readonly code: 'no-session' | 'nothing-accepted' | 'unknown-claim') {
    super(message);
    this.name = 'ReviseError';
  }
}
```

Hmm, careful: `readonly code` in constructor param — erasable syntax? TypeScript parameter properties are not erasable. Node's type stripping supports... Actually parameter properties are NOT supported by type stripping (they generate code). Must avoid. I'll declare field explicitly.

Then:

```ts
export function reviseSession(db: Db, sessionId: string, acceptedIds: readonly string[]): RevisedView
```

Steps:
- session = getSession; if null throw ReviseError('会话不存在', 'no-session')
- dedupe acceptedIds, filter empty
- if length === 0 throw ReviseError('先勾选要采纳的建议：没有勾选就没有要改的地方。', 'nothing-accepted')
- claims = listClaims(db, sessionId); byId map
- unknown = acceptedIds.filter(id => !byId.has(id)); if unknown.length > 0 throw ReviseError('这场会话里没有这些主张：...','unknown-claim')
- revisions: Revision[] = []; skipped: RevisionSkip[] = []
- for each id in acceptedIds order: claim.suggestion null → skipped.push({claimId, reason:'这条主张没有可采纳的修改建议'}); else revisions.push(revisionOf(id, claim.suggestion))
- result = applyRevisions(session.resumeText, revisions)
- accepted = result.applied; skippedAll = [...skipped, ...result.skipped]
- appliedIds = accepted.map(r => r.claimId)
- suggestible = claims.filter(c => c.suggestion !== null).map(c => c.id)
- pending = pendingClaimIds(suggestible, appliedIds)
- text = result.text, digest = reviseDigest(text)
- persist in transaction: clearRevisions; accepted.forEach((rev, i) => insertRevision(db, sessionId, rev, i)); saveRevisedResume(...)
- return view

Key exports:
```ts
export interface RevisedView {
  text: string;
  digest: string;
  accepted: string[];
  pending: string[];
  skipped: RevisionSkip[];
  /** 采纳的每条在新文本里的区间，界面用来高亮 */
  spans: Revision[];
  updatedAt: string;
}

export interface ReviseOutcome {
  view: RevisedView;
  /** 本次请求里被跳过的，和 view.skipped 同源但只含这次点名的 */
  requested: string[];
}

export function reviseOf(db, sessionId): RevisedView | null
export function acceptRevisions(db, sessionId, claimIds): { ok: true; view: RevisedView } | { ok: false; code: number; error: string }
export function clearRevisionsOf(db, sessionId): boolean
```

Hmm, `clearRevisions` name clashes with repo's. Import as `clearRevisions as clearRevisionRows`.

For `pending`: claims that have a suggestion but not accepted. `pendingClaimIds(all, accepted)`.

Now about spans: compute with movedSpans(applied) — but I need applied from the persisted rows. `listRevisions` returns rows in ordinal order = original order. applyRevisions returns applied in original order (升序). So spans = movedSpans(applied). But note: the persisted rows are already the applied ones. So on read, compute movedSpans(listRevisions). Good — and text is stored.

But wait: movedSpans computes end = start + after.length. For stored rows that's fine.

Let me write the code.

Also need the `ReviseOutcome` to give per-request reasons for claims with no suggestion: those become skipped with reason '这条主张没有改写建议'. But applyRevisions' skipped only covers the ones passed in. So I should build the revision list and pre-filter.

Implementation of acceptRevisions:
```ts
const session = getSession(db, sessionId);
if (session === null) return { ok: false, code: 404, error: '会话不存在' };
const claims = listClaims(db, sessionId);
const byId = new Map(claims.map(c => [c.id, c]));
const candidates: Revision[] = [];
const skips: RevisionSkip[] = [];
for (const id of claimIds) {
  const claim = byId.get(id);
  if (claim === undefined) { skips.push({claimId: id, reason: '这条主张不在这份简历里'}); continue; }
  if (claim.suggestion === null) { skips.push({claimId: id, reason: '这条主张没有改写建议'}); continue; }
  candidates.push(revisionOf(id, claim.suggestion));
}
const result = applyRevisions(session.resumeText, candidates);
const skipped = [...skips, ...result.skipped]; // order: requested order first?
```

```text
1 /**
2  * 采纳改写建议：把「改前 / 改后」真正落到简历文本上。
3  *
4  * 三条硬约束，写在代码里而不是写在文档里：
5  *
6  * 1. **只认原文区间，不认客户端传来的文本。** 采纳请求只带 claimId，改前改后
7  *    一律从数据库里那份建议读出来；否则「把简历改一遍」就变成了一个接受任意
8  *    字符串替换的接口。
9  * 2. **区间必须落在原文上。** 用 isGrounded 复核一次，锚不上的建议一律跳过并
10  *    给出理由，绝不「按记忆里的位置硬替换」——一次错位会把整份简历改乱。
11  * 3. **倒序应用。** 一条建议改完，它后面的所有字符位置都会平移。从后往前改，
12  *    前面的区间就不用重算；同时用一个右边界护栏挡住区间重叠的两条建议，
13  *    保证结果与「先改哪条」无关。
14  */
15

21 export interface Revision {
22   claimId: string;
23   start: number;
24   end: number;
25   before: string;
26   after: string;

28
```

```text
1 /**
2  * 简历原文的高亮切分。
3  *
4  * 纯函数、无 React 依赖，因为「哪一段原文被引用过」是产品的核心可视化，
5  * 值得单独测（见 packages/web/test/highlight.test.ts）。
6  */
7 export interface Range {
8   start: number;
9   end: number;
10 }
11 
12 export type SegmentKind = 'plain' | 'hit' | 'focus';
13 
14 export interface Segment {
15   text: string;
16   kind: SegmentKind;
17 }
18 
19 export function clampRange(range: Range, length: number): Range | null {
20   const start = Math.max(0, Math.min(range.start, length));
21   const end = Math.max(0, Math.min(range.end, length));
22   return end > start ? { start, end } : null;
23 }
24 
25 /** 把重叠或相邻的区间并起来，避免在原文上叠出一堆同色小块 */
26 export function mergeRanges(ranges: Range[]): Range[] {
27   const sorted = [...ranges].sort((a, b) => a.start - b.start || a.end - b.end);
28   const merged: Range[] = [];
29   for (const range of sorted) {
30     const last = merged[merged.length - 1];
31     if (last !== undefined && range.start <= last.end) {
32       if (range.end > last.end) last.end = range.end;
33       continue;
34     }
35     merged.push({ start: range.start, end: range.end });
36   }
37   return merged;
38 }
39
```
